      <div class="section" id="stat.85.0155">
        <h1 class="shn">85.0155 LAKE SUPERIOR WATER TRAIL.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.0155.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Creation.</span></h2>
          <p>A water trail is created along the Lake Superior shoreline from Park Point in Duluth to the border with Canada. The trail must be primarily developed for kayakers and campers, using existing public lands for designated rest areas.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.0155.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Commissioner's duties.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of natural resources must coordinate the creation of the water trail by placing signs for rest areas along the lake and working with other public agencies and private resorts owning land along the lake to do the same. At the earliest opportunity, the commissioner shall make available a water trail map depicting the designated rest areas for the touring public.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.0155.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Gifts; donations.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of natural resources is authorized to accept donations of land, or easements in land, for rest areas along the Lake Superior water trail, and may seek and accept money for this purpose from other public and private sources.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.85.0155.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/143/#laws.0.1.0">1993 c 143 s 1</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.85.0156">
        <h1 class="shn">85.0156 MISSISSIPPI WHITEWATER TRAIL.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.0156.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Creation.</span></h2>
          <p>An urban whitewater trail is created along the Mississippi River in the St. Anthony Falls area in Minneapolis. The trail must be primarily developed for whitewater rafters, canoers, and kayakers.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.0156.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Commissioner's duties.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner of natural resources must coordinate the creation of the Whitewater Trail by placing designation signs near and along the river and must publicize the designation.</p>
          <p>(b) In designating the Mississippi Whitewater Trail, the commissioner must work with other federal, state, and local agencies and private businesses and organizations interested in the trail.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.0156.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Gifts; donations.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of natural resources is authorized to accept, on behalf of a nonprofit corporation, donations of land or easements in land for the Whitewater Trail and may seek and accept money for the trail from other public and private sources.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.85.0156.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/401/#laws.0.26.0">1998 c 401 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/13/#laws.0.3.0">1Sp2003 c 13 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.85.016">
        <h1 class="shn">85.016 BICYCLE TRAIL PROGRAM.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner of natural resources must establish a program for the development of bicycle trails utilizing the state trails authorized by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/85.015">85.015</a>, other state parks and recreation land, and state forests. "Bicycle trail," as used in this section, has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.011">169.011</a>. The program must be coordinated with the local park trail-grant program established by the commissioner pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/85.019">85.019</a>, with the state bicycle routes established by the commissioner of transportation pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/160.266">160.266</a>, and with existing and proposed local bikeways. In the metropolitan area as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/473.121">473.121</a>, the program must be developed in accordance with plans and priorities established by the Metropolitan Council. The commissioner must provide technical assistance to local units of government in planning and developing bicycle trails in local parks. The bicycle trail program must, as a minimum, describe the location, design, construction, maintenance, and land acquisition needs of each component trail. The program must be developed after consultation with the state trail council and regional and local units of government and bicyclist organizations.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.85.016.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/166/#laws.0.7.0">1976 c 166 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/199/#laws.0.17.0">1976 c 199 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/421/#laws.0.3.0">1977 c 421 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/356/#laws.0.102.0">1981 c 356 s 102</a>,248; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/289/#laws.0.115.0">1983 c 289 s 115</a> subd 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/558/#laws.4.10.0">1984 c 558 art 4 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/255/#laws.0.1.0">1987 c 255 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/312/#laws.1.26.0">1987 c 312 art 1 s 26</a> subd 2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/186/#laws.0.24.0">1995 c 186 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/3/#laws.3.2.0">1Sp2017 c 3 art 3 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.85.017">
        <h1 class="shn">85.017 TRAIL REGISTRY.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner of natural resources shall compile and maintain a current registry of cross-country skiing, hiking, horseback riding and snowmobiling trails in the state and shall publish and distribute the information in the manner prescribed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/86A.11">86A.11</a>. The Metropolitan Council, the commissioner of employment and economic development, the Minnesota Historical Society, and local units of government shall cooperate with and assist the commissioner in preparing the registry.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.85.017.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/421/#laws.0.4.0">1977 c 421 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/356/#laws.0.103.0">1981 c 356 s 103</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/289/#laws.0.115.0">1983 c 289 s 115</a> subd 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/312/#laws.1.26.0">1987 c 312 art 1 s 26</a> subd 2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/4/#laws.0.1.0">1Sp2003 c 4 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.85.018">
        <h1 class="shn">85.018 TRAIL USE; VEHICLES REGULATED, RESTRICTED.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.018.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of this section:</p>
          <p>(a) "All-terrain vehicle" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/84.92#stat.84.92.8">84.92, subdivision 8</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) "Commissioner" means the commissioner of the state agency from which the grants-in-aid are received.</p>
          <p>(c) "Off-road vehicle" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/84.797#stat.84.797.7">84.797, subdivision 7</a>.</p>
          <p>(d) "Snowmobile" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/84.81#stat.84.81.3">84.81, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
          <p>(e) "Trail" means a recreational trail that is funded in whole or in part by state grants-in-aid to a local unit of government.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.018.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Authority of local government.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A local government unit that receives state grants-in-aid for any trail, with the concurrence of the commissioner, and the landowner or land lessee, may:</p>
          <p>(1) designate the trail for use by snowmobiles or for nonmotorized use from December 1 to April 1 of any year; and</p>
          <p>(2) issue any permit required under subdivisions 3 to 5.</p>
          <p>(b) A local government unit that receives state grants-in-aid under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/84.794#stat.84.794.2">84.794, subdivision 2</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/84.803#stat.84.803.2">84.803, subdivision 2</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/84.927#stat.84.927.2">84.927, subdivision 2</a>, for any trail, with the concurrence of the commissioner, and landowner or land lessee, may:</p>
          <p>(1) designate the trail specifically for use at various times of the year by all-terrain or off-road vehicles or off-highway motorcycles, for nonmotorized use such as ski touring, snowshoeing, and hiking, and for multiple use; and</p>
          <p>(2) issue any permit required under subdivisions 3 to 5.</p>
          <p>(c) A local unit of government that receives state grants-in-aid for any trail, with the concurrence of the commissioner and landowner or land lessee, may designate certain trails for joint use by snowmobiles, off-highway motorcycles, all-terrain vehicles, and off-road vehicles.</p>
          <p>(d) A local unit of government may not prohibit or otherwise restrict operation of an electric-assisted bicycle, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.011#stat.169.011.27">169.011, subdivision 27</a>, on any trail under this section designated for bicycle use or nonmotorized use that includes bicycles, unless the local unit of government determines that operation of the electric-assisted bicycle is not consistent with (1) the safety or general welfare of trail users; or (2) the terms of any property conveyance.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.018.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Motorized use; permits, restrictions.</span></h2>
          <p>Permits may be issued for motorized vehicles, other than those designated, to use a trail designated for use by snowmobiles, off-highway motorcycles, all-terrain or off-road vehicles. Notice of the permit must be conspicuously posted, at the expense of the permit holder, at no less than one-half mile intervals along the trail, for the duration of the permit. Permits shall require that permit holders return the trail and any associated facility to their original condition if any damage is done by the permittee. Limited permits for special events such as races may be issued and shall require the removal of any trail markers, banners and other material used in connection with the special event.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.018.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Nonmotorized use trails.</span></h2>
          <p>No motorized vehicle shall be operated on a trail designated for nonmotorized use. This subdivision does not apply to (1) motorized wheelchairs or other motorized devices operated by an individual who is physically disabled; or (2) electric-assisted bicycles, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.011#stat.169.011.27">169.011, subdivision 27</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.018.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Motorized vehicle trails restricted.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) From December 1 to April 1 in any year no use of a motorized vehicle other than a snowmobile, unless authorized by permit, lease, or easement, shall be permitted on a trail designated for use by snowmobiles.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) No use of a motorized vehicle other than an all-terrain or off-road vehicle and an off-highway motorcycle, unless authorized by permit, lease, or easement, shall be permitted on a trail designated for use by all-terrain vehicles, off-road vehicles, or both, and off-highway motorcycles.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.018.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Exceptions.</span></h2>
          <p>The following motor vehicles are exempt from the provisions of subdivisions 3 to 5:</p>
          <p>(1) military, fire, emergency or law enforcement vehicles used for official or emergency purposes;</p>
          <p>(2) vehicles registered to the county, state or federal government;</p>
          <p>(3) vehicles authorized by permit, lease or contract;</p>
          <p>(4) vehicles owned by private persons engaged in the upkeep and maintenance of the trail systems under the direction of the local unit of government that manages the trail; and</p>
          <p>(5) vehicles registered to or operated with the permission of a landowner on whose lands the trail system has been constructed, but only with respect to operation on the land of that owner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.018.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Streets and highways.</span></h2>
          <p>This section does not apply to any portion of a trail located on any street or highway as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.011">169.011</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.018.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Enforcement.</span></h2>
          <p>The provisions of this section may be enforced by officers of the Department of Natural Resources as provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.201">97A.201</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.235">97A.235</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.85.018.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/215/#laws.0.2.0">1981 c 215 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/609/#laws.0.5.0">1984 c 609 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/386/#laws.4.17.0">1986 c 386 art 4 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/452/#laws.0.16.0">1986 c 452 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/311/#laws.1.11.0">1993 c 311 art 1 s 11</a>-13; art 2 s 10-13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/95/#laws.0.2.0">1999 c 95 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/0/107/#laws.0.36.0">2011 c 107 s 36</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/272/#laws.0.16.0">2012 c 272 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/287/#laws.4.2.0">2012 c 287 art 4 s 2</a>,3</p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">ADMINISTRATION</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.85.019">
        <h1 class="shn">85.019 LOCAL RECREATION GRANTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.019.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definition.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of this section, "unit of government" means a county, statutory or home rule charter city, or town.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.019.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Parks and outdoor recreation areas.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall administer a program to provide grants to units of government for up to 50 percent of the costs of acquisition and betterment of public land and improvements needed for parks and other outdoor recreation areas and facilities, including costs to create veterans memorial gardens and parks.</p>
          <p>(b) For units of government outside the metropolitan area as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/473.121">473.121</a>, subdivision 2, the local match required for a grant to acquire or better a regional park or regional outdoor recreation area is $2 of nonstate money for each $3 of state money.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.019.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed by amendment, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/220/#laws.0.68.0">1995 c 220 s 68</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.019.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed by amendment, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/220/#laws.0.68.0">1995 c 220 s 68</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.019.4a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Natural and scenic areas.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall administer a program to provide grants to units of government and school districts for the acquisition and betterment of natural and scenic areas such as blufflands, prairies, shorelands, wetlands, and wooded areas. A grant may not exceed 50 percent or $500,000, whichever is less, of the costs of acquisition and betterment of land acquired under this subdivision. The commissioner shall make payment to a unit of government upon receiving documentation of reimbursable expenditures.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.019.4b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4b.<span class="headnote">Regional trails.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The commissioner shall administer a program to provide grants to units of government for acquisition and betterment of public land and improvements needed for trails outside the metropolitan area deemed to be of regional significance according to criteria published by the commissioner. Recipients must provide a nonstate cash match of at least 25 percent of total eligible project costs. If land used for the trails is not in full public ownership, then the recipients must prove it is dedicated to the purposes of the grants for at least 20 years. The commissioner shall make payment to a unit of government upon receiving documentation of reimbursable expenditures. A unit of government may enter into a lease or management agreement for the trail, subject to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.695">16A.695</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.019.4c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4c.<span class="headnote">Trail connections.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The commissioner shall administer a program to provide grants to units of government for acquisition and betterment of public land and improvements needed for trails that connect communities, trails, and parks and thereby increase the effective length of trail experiences. Recipients must provide a nonstate cash match of at least 25 percent of total eligible project costs. If land used for the trails is not in full public ownership, then the recipients must prove it is dedicated to the purposes of the grants for at least 20 years. The commissioner shall make payment to a unit of government upon receiving documentation of reimbursable expenditures. A unit of government may enter into a lease or management agreement for the trail, subject to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.695">16A.695</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.019.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Powers; rules.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner has all powers necessary and convenient to implement this section, including the authority to adopt rules for the program under chapter 14.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.019.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Administering grants.</span></h2>
          <p>Up to 2.5 percent of appropriations for grants under this section from revenue deposited in the natural resources fund under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.94">297A.94</a>, paragraph (h), clause (4), may be used by the commissioner for the actual costs of administering the grants.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.85.019.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/172/#laws.0.35.0">1993 c 172 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/643/#laws.0.48.0">1994 c 643 s 48</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/220/#laws.0.68.0">1995 c 220 s 68</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/407/#laws.0.35.0">1996 c 407 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/404/#laws.0.35.0">1998 c 404 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/231/#laws.0.102.0">1999 c 231 s 102</a>-104; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/492/#laws.1.40.0">2000 c 492 art 1 s 40</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/393/#laws.0.42.0">2002 c 393 s 42</a>,43; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/20/#laws.1.31.0">2005 c 20 art 1 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/258/#laws.0.32.0">2006 c 258 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/0/107/#laws.0.37.0">2011 c 107 s 37</a>,38; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/6/#laws.2.32.0">1Sp2021 c 6 art 2 s 32</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.85.02"><b>85.02</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1943/0/60/#laws.0.12.0">1943 c 60 s 12</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.85.021">
        <h1 class="shn">85.021 ACQUIRING LAND; MINNESOTA VALLEY TRAIL.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.021.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Acquiring entire tract.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of natural resources on determining that it is necessary to acquire any interest in a part of a tract or parcel of real estate for purposes of the Minnesota Valley Trail, may acquire in fee the whole or any additional parts of the tract or parcel that the commissioner deems to be in the best interests of the state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.021.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Conveying excess.</span></h2>
          <p>Within one year after acquiring excess real estate pursuant to subdivision 1, the commissioner of natural resources shall notify the governor that the excess real estate is available for sale. The commissioner shall then publish notice of sale for three successive weeks in a newspaper of general circulation in the territory from which bids are likely to be received. After receipt of sealed bids, and upon recommendation of the commissioner of natural resources, the governor shall convey the excess real estate by quitclaim deed in a form approved by the attorney general in the name of the state to the highest responsible bidder. The deed may contain restrictive clauses limiting the use of the real estate in the interest of preserving the integrity of the trail when the commissioner finds that the restrictions are reasonably necessary.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.021.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Leasing.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may lease for the term between the acquisition and sale thereof and for a fair rental rate and upon terms and conditions that the commissioner deems proper, any excess real estate acquired under the provisions of this section and any real estate acquired in fee for natural resources purposes and not presently needed therefor. All rents received from the leases shall be paid into the state treasury.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.85.021.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/144/#laws.0.2.0">1975 c 144 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/458/#laws.0.13.0">1980 c 458 s 13</a>,14; 1986 c 444</p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.85.03"><b>85.03</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/524/#laws.0.5.0">1969 c 524 s 5</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.85.04">
        <h1 class="shn">85.04 ENFORCEMENT DIVISION EMPLOYEES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.04.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Peace officer employment.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of natural resources may employ peace officers as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.84#stat.626.84.1">626.84, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (c), to enforce laws governing the use of state parks, state monuments, state recreation areas, and state waysides.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.04.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Other employees.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of natural resources may designate certain employees according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/84.0835">84.0835</a> to enforce laws governing the use of state parks, state monuments, state recreation areas, state waysides, state forest lands when incidental to normal forestry duties, and state forest subareas.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.85.04.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/40">6461</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1923/0/430/#laws.0.9.0">1923 c 430 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/525/#laws.0.1.0">1969 c 525 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/128/#laws.1.42.0">2003 c 128 art 1 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/260/#laws.0.8.0">2004 c 260 s 8</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.85.041"><b>85.041</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/384/#laws.0.3.0">1980 c 384 s 3</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.85.045">
        <h1 class="shn">85.045 ADOPT-A-PARK PROGRAM.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.045.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Creation.</span></h2>
          <p>The Minnesota adopt-a-park program is established. The commissioner shall coordinate the program through the regional offices of the Department of Natural Resources.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.045.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Purpose.</span></h2>
          <p>The purpose of the program is to encourage business and civic groups or individuals to assist, on a volunteer basis, in improving and maintaining state parks, state recreation areas, monuments, historic sites, and trails.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.045.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Agreements.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall enter into informal agreements with business and civic groups or individuals for volunteer services to maintain and make improvements to real and personal property in state parks, monuments, historic sites, and trails in accordance with plans devised by the commissioner after consultation with the groups.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner may erect appropriate signs to recognize and express appreciation to groups and individuals providing volunteer services under the adopt-a-park program.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner may provide assistance to enhance the comfort and safety of volunteers and to facilitate the implementation and administration of the adopt-a-park program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.045.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Worker displacement prohibited.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may not enter into any agreement that has the purpose of or results in the displacement of public employees by volunteers participating in the adopt-a-park program under this section. The commissioner must certify to the appropriate bargaining agent that the work performed by a volunteer will not result in the displacement of currently employed workers or workers on seasonal layoff or layoff from a substantially equivalent position, including partial displacement such as reduction in hours of nonovertime work, wages, or other employment benefits.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.85.045.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/242/#laws.0.1.0">1991 c 242 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/172/#laws.0.36.0">1993 c 172 s 36</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/187/#laws.3.22.0">1997 c 187 art 3 s 22</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.85.05"><b>85.05</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/253/#laws.0.5.0">1987 c 253 s 5</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.85.0505">
        <h1 class="shn">85.0505 FOOD AND BEVERAGE SERVICE IN STATE PARKS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.0505.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Itasca State Park.</span></h2>
          <p>Minnesota-produced wine and beer may be sold and consumed by the drink at the restaurant in Douglas Lodge in Itasca State Park, subject to other laws relating to the sale of intoxicating liquor.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.0505.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/176/#laws.1.52.0">2009 c 176 art 1 s 52</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.0505.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>MS 2020 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/6/#laws.2.134.0">1Sp2021 c 6 art 2 s 134</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.85.0505.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/128/#laws.0.6.0">1987 c 128 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/236/#laws.0.2.0">1997 c 236 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/4/#laws.5.1.0">1Sp2017 c 4 art 5 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.85.0506">
        <h1 class="shn">85.0506 LAKE VERMILION-SOUDAN UNDERGROUND MINE STATE PARK; HOISTS.</h1>
        <p>The Lake Vermilion-Soudan Underground Mine State Park mine tour operation is exempt from sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326B.163">326B.163</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326B.191">326B.191</a>. The federal mine code for hoists that lift people under Code of Federal Regulations, title 30, part 57, subpart R, applies to the Lake Vermilion-Soudan Underground Mine State Park hoist. The commissioner shall employ a hoist safety expert to conduct an annual inspection of the hoist system at the Lake Vermilion-Soudan Underground Mine State Park.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.85.0506.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/1/4/#laws.4.36.0">1Sp2015 c 4 art 4 s 36</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.85.0507"><b>85.0507</b> MS 2020 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/6/#laws.2.134.0">1Sp2021 c 6 art 2 s 134</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.85.051"><b>85.051</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/335/#laws.4.109.0">1989 c 335 art 4 s 109</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.85.052">
        <h1 class="shn">85.052 USES OF STATE PARKS; FEES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.052.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Authority to establish.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner may establish, by written order, provisions for the use of state parks for the following:</p>
          <p>(1) special parking space for automobiles or other motor-driven vehicles in a state park or state recreation area;</p>
          <p>(2) special parking spurs, campgrounds for automobiles, sites for tent camping, other types of lodging, camping, or day use facilities, and special auto trailer coach parking spaces, for the use of the individual charged for the space or facility;</p>
          <p>(3) providing water, sewer, and electric service to trailer or tent campsites and charging a reasonable use fee; and</p>
          <p>(4) administrative penalties related to courtesy warnings and letters issued for failure to display a state park permit as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/85.053#stat.85.053.2">85.053, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) Provisions established under paragraph (a) are exempt from section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.1283">16A.1283</a> and the rulemaking provisions of chapter 14. Section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.386">14.386</a> does not apply.</p>
          <p>(c) For the purposes of this subdivision, "lodging" means an enclosed shelter, room, or building with furnishings for overnight use.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.052.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">State park special events.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner may stage state park special events in a state park, municipal park, or on other land near or adjoining a state park and charge an entrance or use fee for the special event. All receipts from the special events must be used in the same manner as though the special events were conducted in a state park.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner may establish, by written order, state park special event areas to hold historical or other special events conducted by the commissioner of a state agency or other public agency. Establishment of the areas is exempt from the rulemaking provisions of chapter 14, and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.386">14.386</a> does not apply.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.052.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Fee for certain parking and campsite use.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) An individual using spaces in state parks under subdivision 1, clause (2), shall be charged daily rates determined and set by the commissioner in a manner and amount consistent with the type of facility provided for the accommodation of guests in a particular park and with similar facilities offered for tourist camping and similar use in the area.</p>
          <p>(b) The fee for special parking spurs, campgrounds for automobiles, sites for tent camping, and special auto trailer coach parking spaces is one-half of the fee set in paragraph (a) on Sunday through Thursday of each week for a physically disabled person:</p>
          <p>(1) with a motor vehicle that has disability plates issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/168.021#stat.168.021.1">168.021, subdivision 1</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) who possesses a certificate issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.345">169.345</a>; or</p>
          <p>(3) who possesses an interagency access pass for state residents with permanent disabilities, issued by the federal government under the Federal Lands Recreation Enhancement Act.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.052.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Deposit of fees.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Fees paid for providing contracted products and services within a state park, state recreation area, or wayside, and for special state park uses under this section shall be deposited in the natural resources fund and credited to a state parks account.</p>
          <p>(b) Gross receipts derived from sales, rentals, or leases of natural resources within state parks, recreation areas, and waysides, other than those on trust fund lands, must be deposited in the state treasury and credited to the state parks working capital account.</p>
          <p>(c) Notwithstanding paragraph (b), the gross receipts from the sale of stockpile materials, aggregate, or other earth materials from the Iron Range Off-Highway Vehicle Recreation Area shall be deposited in the dedicated accounts in the natural resources fund from which the purchase of the stockpile material was made. Notwithstanding paragraph (b), the payments made under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/93.22#stat.93.22.1">93.22, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (c), pursuant to a state mineral lease on lands and mineral rights purchased for and within the Iron Range Off-Highway Vehicle Recreation Area shall be deposited in the dedicated accounts in the natural resources fund from which the purchase of the lands was made.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.052.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Establishing fees.</span></h2>
          <p>Except as otherwise specified in law, and notwithstanding sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.1283">16A.1283</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.1285#stat.16A.1285.2">16A.1285, subdivision 2</a>, the commissioner shall, by written order, establish fees providing for the use of state parks and state recreation areas. The fees are not subject to the rulemaking provisions of chapter 14, and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.386">14.386</a> does not apply.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.052.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">State park reservation system.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner may, by written order, develop reasonable reservation policies for using camping, lodging, and day-use facilities and for tours, educational programs, seminars, events, and rentals. The policies are exempt from the rulemaking provisions under chapter 14, and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.386">14.386 </a>does not apply.</p>
          <p>(b) The revenue collected from the state park reservation fee established under subdivision 5, including interest earned, must be deposited in the state park account in the natural resources fund and is annually appropriated to the commissioner for the cost of operating the state park reservation and point-of-sale system.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.052.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Special-use permits.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may, by written order, develop reasonable policies for special-use permits to use state parks, state recreation areas, and state waysides. The policies are exempt from the rulemaking provisions under chapter 14, and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.386">14.386</a> does not apply.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.85.052.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/253/#laws.0.1.0">1987 c 253 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/335/#laws.4.106.0">1989 c 335 art 4 s 106</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/2/#laws.0.86.0">1Sp2001 c 2 s 86</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/128/#laws.1.43.0">2003 c 128 art 1 s 43</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/221/#laws.0.16.0">2004 c 221 s 16</a>-19; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/255/#laws.0.14.0">2004 c 255 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/64/#laws.0.1.0">2005 c 64 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/281/#laws.3.3.0">2006 c 281 art 3 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/361/#laws.4.29.0">2010 c 361 art 4 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/0/98/#laws.0.1.0">2011 c 98 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/2/#laws.4.4.0">1Sp2011 c 2 art 4 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/272/#laws.0.17.0">2012 c 272 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/114/#laws.4.15.0">2013 c 114 art 4 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/93/#laws.2.30.0">2017 c 93 art 2 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/6/#laws.2.33.0">1Sp2021 c 6 art 2 s 33</a>-36; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/60/#laws.4.21.0">2023 c 60 art 4 s 21</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.85.053">
        <h1 class="shn">85.053 STATE PARK PERMITS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.053.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Form, issuance, validity.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall prepare and provide state park permits for each calendar year that state a motor vehicle may enter and use state parks, state recreation areas, and state waysides over 50 acres in area. State park permits must be available and placed on sale by January 1 of the calendar year that the permit is valid. A separate motorcycle permit may be prepared and provided by the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(b) An annual state park permit may be used from the time it is purchased for a 12-month period. State park permits in each category must be numbered consecutively for each year of issue.</p>
          <p>(c) State park permits shall be issued by employees of the Division of Parks and Recreation as designated by the commissioner. State park permits also may be consigned to and issued by agents designated by the commissioner who are not employees of the Division of Parks and Recreation. All proceeds from the sale of permits and all unsold permits consigned to agents shall be returned to the commissioner at such times as the commissioner may direct, but no later than the end of the calendar year for which the permits are effective. No part of the permit fee may be retained by an agent. An additional charge or fee in an amount to be determined by the commissioner, but not to exceed four percent of the price of the permit, may be collected and retained by an agent for handling or selling the permits.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.053.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Requirement.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Except as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/85.054">85.054</a>, a motor vehicle may not enter a state park, state recreation area, or state wayside over 50 acres in area, without a state park permit issued under this section or a state parks and trails plate issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/168.1295">168.1295</a>. Except for vehicles permitted under subdivisions 7, paragraph (a), clause (2), and 8, the state park permit must be affixed to the lower right corner windshield of the motor vehicle and must be completely affixed by its own adhesive to the windshield, or the commissioner may, by written order, provide an alternative means to display and validate state park permits. A motor vehicle owner or lessee is responsible for ensuring the owner's or lessee's vehicle has a state park permit, and the commissioner may issue warnings and citations under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/84.0835">84.0835</a> to the owner or lessee of a vehicle not in compliance.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.053.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Split Rock Lighthouse State Park.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding subdivision 2, the commissioner of natural resources may negotiate with the Minnesota Historical Society a special fee structure for the Split Rock Lighthouse state historic site within Split Rock Lighthouse State Park.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.053.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Multiple-vehicle permits.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall prescribe and issue multiple-vehicle state park permits for persons who own more than one motor vehicle and who request the permit for additional vehicles on a form prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.053.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">One-day permits.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall prescribe a special state park permit for use of state parks, state recreation areas, or state waysides for one day under conditions prescribed by the commissioner. For campers, the permit shall allow for the use of the park where camped from the time of purchase until the camping check-out time of the following day.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.053.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Daily vehicle permit for groups.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall prescribe a special daily vehicle state park permit for groups for use of state parks, state recreation areas, or state waysides for up to one day under conditions prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.053.5a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5a.<span class="headnote">Free permit; members of federally recognized tribes.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner must issue an annual state park permit for no charge to any member of the 11 federally recognized tribes in Minnesota. To qualify for a free state park permit under this subdivision, an individual must present a qualifying tribal identification, as determined by each of the tribal governments, to the park attendant on duty or other designee of the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(b) For vehicles permitted under paragraph (a), the permit issued under this subdivision is valid only when displayed on a vehicle owned and occupied by the person to whom the permit is issued.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner may issue a daily state park permit free of charge to an individual who qualifies under paragraph (a) and does not own or operate a motor vehicle.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.053.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Employee's permit.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall prescribe and issue an employee's state park permit to state employees, peace officers, and contractors, that must enter areas where state park permits are required to perform official duties. An employee, peace officer, or contractor must display the special permit on the motor vehicle in the same manner as state park permits are displayed.</p>
          <p>(b) A motor vehicle displaying only an employee's state park permit may not enter a place where state park permits are required if the vehicle is used for purposes other than performing official duties.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.053.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Disabled persons.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall prescribe and issue special state park permits for:</p>
          <p>(1) a physically disabled person with a motor vehicle (i) that has disability plates issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/168.021#stat.168.021.1">168.021, subdivision 1</a>, or (ii) who has a permanent disability certificate issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.345">169.345</a> and who can demonstrate proof of ownership of the vehicle for which the state park permit is being purchased or proof of a leasehold interest in the vehicle for a term at least as long as the term of the permit;</p>
          <p>(2) a physically disabled person who: (i) does not own or operate a motor vehicle; (ii) possesses a statement certified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.345#stat.169.345.2a">169.345, subdivision 2a</a>; and (iii) applies to the commissioner in writing; and</p>
          <p>(3) a permanently disabled person who possesses an interagency access pass for people with permanent disabilities, issued by the federal government under the Federal Lands Recreation Enhancement Act.</p>
          <p>(b) For vehicles permitted under paragraph (a), clause (1), the permit or the decal issued under this subdivision is valid only when displayed on a vehicle owned and occupied by the person to whom the permit is issued.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.053.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Free permit; military personnel.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Annual permits under subdivision 1 must be issued without a fee to active military personnel in any branch or unit of the United States armed forces or their dependents and to recipients of a Purple Heart medal. To qualify for a free permit under this subdivision, a person must present qualifying military identification or an annual pass for the United States military issued through the National Parks and Federal Recreational Lands Pass program to the park attendant on duty or other designee of the commissioner.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) For purposes of this subdivision, the commissioner shall establish what constitutes qualifying military identification in the State Register.</p>
          <p>(c) For vehicles permitted under paragraph (a), the permit or decal issued under this subdivision is valid only when displayed on a vehicle owned and occupied by the person to whom the permit is issued.</p>
          <p>(d) The commissioner may issue a daily vehicle permit free of charge to an individual who qualifies under paragraph (a) and does not own or operate a motor vehicle.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.053.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Towed vehicles.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall prescribe and issue a temporary permit for a vehicle that enters a park towed by a vehicle used for camping. The temporary permit shall be issued with the camping permit and allows the towed vehicle to be driven in state parks until the camping permit expires.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.053.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Free permit; disabled veterans.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The commissioner shall issue an annual park permit for no charge to any veteran with any level of service-connected disability, as determined by the United States Department of Veterans Affairs, who presents each year a copy of the veteran's determination letter or other official form of validation issued by the United States Department of Veterans Affairs or the United States Department of Defense to a park attendant or commissioner's designee. For the purposes of this subdivision, "veteran" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/197.447">197.447</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) For vehicles permitted under paragraph (a), the permit or decal issued under this subdivision is valid only when displayed on a vehicle owned and occupied by the person to whom the permit is issued.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The commissioner may issue a daily vehicle permit free of charge to an individual who qualifies under paragraph (a) and does not own or operate a motor vehicle.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.85.053.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/253/#laws.0.2.0">1987 c 253 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/137/#laws.0.1.0">1989 c 137 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/427/#laws.0.1.0">1990 c 427 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/254/#laws.2.15.0">1991 c 254 art 2 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/481/#laws.0.1.0">1992 c 481 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/458/#laws.0.1.0">1994 c 458 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/407/#laws.0.36.0">1996 c 407 s 36</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/278/#laws.0.1.0">2000 c 278 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/128/#laws.1.44.0">2003 c 128 art 1 s 44</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/228/#laws.1.19.0">2004 c 228 art 1 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/64/#laws.0.2.0">2005 c 64 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/1/#laws.2.50.0">1Sp2005 c 1 art 2 s 50</a>,51; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/236/#laws.1.2.0">2006 c 236 art 1 s 2</a>;<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/273/#laws.0.2.0"> 2006 c 273 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/281/#laws.3.4.0">2006 c 281 art 3 s 4</a>,5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/13/#laws.2.19.0">2007 c 13 art 2 s 19</a>;<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/131/#laws.1.17.0"> 2007 c 131 art 1 s 17</a>,18,96; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/357/#laws.0.17.0">2008 c 357 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/37/#laws.1.21.0">2009 c 37 art 1 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/176/#laws.1.12.0">2009 c 176 art 1 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/361/#laws.4.30.0">2010 c 361 art 4 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/272/#laws.0.18.0">2012 c 272 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/114/#laws.4.16.0">2013 c 114 art 4 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.13.14.0">2014 c 312 art 13 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/93/#laws.2.31.0">2017 c 93 art 2 s 31</a>,32; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/6/#laws.2.37.0">1Sp2021 c 6 art 2 s 37</a>,38</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.85.054">
        <h1 class="shn">85.054 STATE PARK PERMIT EXEMPTIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.054.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">State Park Open House Days.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A state park permit is not required for a motor vehicle to enter a state park, state monument, state recreation area, or state wayside, on four days each calendar year at each park, which the commissioner shall designate as State Park Open House Days. The commissioner may designate two consecutive days as State Park Open House Days, if the open house is held in conjunction with a special event described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/85.052#stat.85.052.2">85.052, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner shall announce the date of each State Park Open House Day at least 30 days in advance of the date it occurs.</p>
          <p>(c) The purpose of State Park Open House Days is to acquaint the public with state parks, recreation areas, and waysides.</p>
          <p>(d) On State Park Open House Days, registered overnight guests in state parks and state recreation areas are exempt from the requirements for a state park permit under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/85.053">85.053</a> until after the camping or lodging check-out time of the following day in the park where the overnight stay occurred.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.054.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Fort Snelling Memorial Chapel Island.</span></h2>
          <p>A state park permit is not required and a fee may not be charged for motor vehicle entry or parking at the Fort Snelling Memorial Chapel Island portion of Fort Snelling State Park.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.054.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/357/#laws.0.40.0">2008 c 357 s 40</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.054.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Jay Cooke State Park.</span></h2>
          <p>A state park permit is not required and a fee must not be charged for motor vehicle entry or parking at the Veterans Memorial scenic overlook portion of Jay Cooke State Park.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.054.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Gooseberry Falls State Park.</span></h2>
          <p>A state park permit is not required and a fee must not be charged for motor vehicle entry or parking at the class I highway rest area parking lot located adjacent to marked Trunk Highway 61 and the Gooseberry River at Gooseberry Falls State Park.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.054.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Iron Range Off-Highway Vehicle Recreation Area.</span></h2>
          <p>A state park permit is not required and a fee may not be charged for motor vehicle entry or parking at the Iron Range Off-Highway Vehicle Recreation Area, except that the commissioner may establish special event fees.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.054.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Tettegouche State Park.</span></h2>
          <p>A state park permit is not required and a fee may not be charged for motor vehicle entry at Palisade Head in Tettegouche State Park, provided that motor vehicles entering the park under this provision may not be parked at Palisade Head for more than one hour. A state park permit is not required and a fee may not be charged for the class I rest area parking area at Tettegouche State Park.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.054.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Zippel Bay State Park.</span></h2>
          <p>A state park permit is not required and a fee may not be charged for motor vehicle entry or parking at the parking area located adjacent to County State-Aid Highway No. 4 at Zippel Bay State Park, Lake of the Woods County.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.054.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Lake Bemidji State Park.</span></h2>
          <p>A state park permit is not required and a fee may not be charged for motor vehicle entry to, use of roads to, or parking at the Department of Natural Resources regional headquarters portion of Lake Bemidji State Park.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.054.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Red River State Recreation Area.</span></h2>
          <p>A state park permit is not required and a fee may not be charged for any portion of the Red River State Recreation Area located outside of the campground.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.054.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Big Bog State Recreation Area.</span></h2>
          <p>A state park permit is not required and a fee may not be charged for motor vehicle entry or parking at the parking area located north of Tamarac River in the southern unit of Big Bog State Recreation Area, Beltrami County.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.054.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Lake Vermilion-Soudan Underground Mine State Park.</span></h2>
          <p>A state park permit is not required and a fee may not be charged for motor vehicle entry or parking at the visitor parking area of Soudan Underground Mine and the Stuntz Bay boat house area.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.054.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Cuyuna Country State Recreation Area.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A state park permit is not required and a fee may not be charged for motor vehicle entry or parking at Croft Mine Historical Park and Portsmouth Mine Lake Overlook in Cuyuna Country State Recreation Area, except for overnight camping.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.054.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Grand Portage State Park.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A state park permit is not required and a fee may not be charged for motor vehicle entry or parking at the class 1 highway rest area parking lot located adjacent to marked Trunk Highway 61 and Pigeon River at Grand Portage State Park.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.054.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">John A. Latsch State Park.</span></h2>
          <p>A state park permit is not required and a fee may not be charged for motor vehicle entry or parking at the parking lot located adjacent to John Latsch Road and Trunk Highway 61 at John A. Latsch State Park.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.054.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Greenleaf Lake State Recreation Area.</span></h2>
          <p>A state park permit is not required and a fee may not be charged for motor vehicle entry or parking at Greenleaf Lake State Recreation Area.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.054.17">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">School-sanctioned activities.</span></h2>
          <p>A state park permit is not required and a fee may not be charged for vehicles transporting K-12 students engaged in school-sanctioned activities at state parks.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.054.18">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">La Salle Lake State Recreation Area.</span></h2>
          <p>A state park permit is not required and a fee may not be charged for motor vehicle entry, use, or parking in La Salle Lake State Recreation Area unless the occupants of the vehicle enter, use, or park in a developed campground, overnight, or day-use area.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.054.19">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.</h2>
          <p>MS 2020 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/6/#laws.2.134.0">1Sp2021 c 6 art 2 s 134</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.85.054.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/253/#laws.0.3.0">1987 c 253 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/448/#laws.0.3.0">1994 c 448 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/215/#laws.0.2.0">1995 c 215 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/407/#laws.0.37.0">1996 c 407 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/236/#laws.0.3.0">1997 c 236 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/391/#laws.0.1.0">1998 c 391 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/376/#laws.0.3.0">2002 c 376 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/255/#laws.0.15.0">2004 c 255 s 15</a>,16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/1/#laws.2.52.0">1Sp2005 c 1 art 2 s 52</a>,53; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/281/#laws.3.6.0">2006 c 281 art 3 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/13/#laws.1.5.0">2007 c 13 art 1 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/57/#laws.1.41.0">2007 c 57 art 1 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/357/#laws.0.18.0">2008 c 357 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/176/#laws.1.13.0">2009 c 176 art 1 s 13</a>-15; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/114/#laws.4.17.0">2013 c 114 art 4 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/1/4/#laws.4.37.0">1Sp2015 c 4 art 4 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/93/#laws.2.33.0">2017 c 93 art 2 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/4/#laws.3.29.0">1Sp2019 c 4 art 3 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/6/#laws.2.39.0">1Sp2021 c 6 art 2 s 39</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.85.055">
        <h1 class="shn">85.055 STATE PARK PERMIT FEES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.055.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Fees.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The fee for state park permits for:</p>
          <p>(1) an annual use of state parks is $35;</p>
          <p>(2) a second or subsequent vehicle state park permit is $26;</p>
          <p>(3) a state park permit valid for one day is $7;</p>
          <p>(4) a daily vehicle state park permit for groups is $5;</p>
          <p>(5) an annual permit for motorcycles is $30;</p>
          <p>(6) an employee's state park permit is without charge; and</p>
          <p>(7) a state park permit for persons with disabilities under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/85.053#stat.85.053.7">85.053, subdivision 7</a>, paragraph (a), clauses (1) to (3), is $12.</p>
          <p>(b) The fees specified in this subdivision include any sales tax required by state law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.055.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Patron permit fee.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.1283">16A.1283</a>, the commissioner of natural resources may, by written order, provide a special patron permit allowing persons to pay an additional amount above the annual permit fee required in subdivision 1. The additional amount paid under this subdivision is not subject to the rulemaking provisions of chapter 14 and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.386">14.386</a> does not apply.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.055.1b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1b.<span class="headnote">Discounts.</span></h2>
          <p>Except as otherwise specified in law, and notwithstanding section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.1285#stat.16A.1285.2">16A.1285, subdivision 2</a>, the commissioner may by written order authorize waiver or reduction of state park entrance fees.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.055.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Fee deposit and appropriation.</span></h2>
          <p>The fees collected under this section shall be deposited in the natural resources fund and credited to the state parks account. Money in the account, except for the electronic licensing system commission established by the commissioner under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/84.027#stat.84.027.15">84.027, subdivision 15</a>, and the state park reservation system fee established by the commissioner under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/85.052">85.052, subdivisions 5</a> and 6, is available for appropriation to the commissioner to operate and maintain the state park system.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.85.055.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/253/#laws.0.4.0">1987 c 253 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/686/#laws.1.55.0">1988 c 686 art 1 s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/335/#laws.4.22.0">1989 c 335 art 4 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/254/#laws.2.16.0">1991 c 254 art 2 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/458/#laws.0.3.0">1994 c 458 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/407/#laws.0.38.0">1996 c 407 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/216/#laws.0.67.0">1997 c 216 s 67</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/0/7/#laws.0.22.0">2001 c 7 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/2/#laws.0.87.0">1Sp2001 c 2 s 87</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/128/#laws.1.45.0">2003 c 128 art 1 s 45</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/221/#laws.0.20.0">2004 c 221 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/1/#laws.2.54.0">1Sp2005 c 1 art 2 s 54</a>,55; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/281/#laws.3.7.0">2006 c 281 art 3 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/176/#laws.1.16.0">2009 c 176 art 1 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/114/#laws.4.18.0">2013 c 114 art 4 s 18</a>,19; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/275/#laws.1.13.0">2014 c 275 art 1 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/93/#laws.2.34.0">2017 c 93 art 2 s 34</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.85.056">
        <h1 class="shn">85.056 STATE PARKS AND TRAILS DONATION ACCOUNT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.056.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Establishment.</span></h2>
          <p>The state parks and trails donation account is established as a separate account in the natural resources fund. The account shall be administered by the commissioner of natural resources as provided in this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.056.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Funding sources.</span></h2>
          <p>The state parks and trails donation account shall consist of contributions made under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/168.1295">168.1295</a> and other contributions. The contributions may be made in cash, property, land, or interests in land.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.056.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Uses.</span></h2>
          <p>Money in the account is appropriated to the commissioner of natural resources to operate and maintain the state parks and trails system.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.85.056.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.13.15.0">2014 c 312 art 13 s 15</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.85.06">
        <h1 class="shn">85.06 SCHOOLHOUSES IN CERTAIN STATE PARKS.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner is hereby authorized to allow the construction and use of schoolhouses in any state park the area of which is not less than 3,000 acres.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.85.06.history

      </div>

      <div class="sr" id="stat.85.198"><b>85.198</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/859/#laws.0.14.0">1971 c 859 s 14</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.85.20">
        <h1 class="shn">85.20 VIOLATIONS OF RULES; LITTERING; PENALTIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.20.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Violation of rules.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Any person who, within the limits of any outdoor recreation unit established in chapter 86A, shall willfully cut, injure, or destroy any live tree, shrub, timber, evergreen, or ornamental plant of any kind, or who shall willfully injure, remove, destroy, deface, or mutilate any guideboard, guidepost, furniture, fixture, improvement, monument, tablet, or other property of the state of any kind, or who shall willfully violate, or fail to comply with, any rule of the commissioner adopted according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/86A.06">86A.06</a>, is guilty of a petty misdemeanor.</p>
          <p>(b) Violations under paragraph (a) adopted for wildlife management areas described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/86A.05#stat.86A.05.8">86A.05, subdivision 8</a>, are misdemeanors, consistent with game and fish law penalties defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.301#stat.97A.301.1">97A.301, subdivision 1</a>, clause (6).</p>
          <p>(c) If a different penalty is provided in another section of law for the violation and the person is charged under that section of law, the penalty specified for the violation will control over the penalty specified in paragraphs (a) and (b). Violations relating to the taking of wild animals are subject to the penalties as specified in the game and fish laws described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.011">97A.011</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.20.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/353/#laws.0.41.0">1975 c 353 s 41</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.20.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/353/#laws.0.41.0">1975 c 353 s 41</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.20.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/353/#laws.0.41.0">1975 c 353 s 41</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.20.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/353/#laws.0.41.0">1975 c 353 s 41</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.85.20.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Littering; penalty.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) No person shall drain, throw, or deposit upon the lands and waters within any unit of the outdoor recreation system as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/86A.04">86A.04</a> any substance, including cigarette filters and debris from fireworks, that would mar the appearance, create a stench, destroy the cleanliness or safety of the land, or would be likely to injure any animal, vehicle, or person traveling upon those lands and waters. The operator of a vehicle or watercraft, except a school bus or a vehicle transporting passengers for hire and regulated by a successor agency of the former Interstate Commerce Commission, shall not permit articles to be thrown or discarded from the vehicle upon any lands or waters within any unit of the outdoor recreation system.</p>
          <p>(b) Violation of this subdivision is a misdemeanor. Any person sentenced under this subdivision shall in lieu of the sentence imposed be permitted, under terms established by the court, to work under the direction of the Department of Natural Resources at clearing rubbish, trash, and debris from any unit of the outdoor recreation system. The court may for any violation of this subdivision order the offender to perform such work under terms established by the court with the option of a jail sentence being imposed.</p>
          <p>(c) In lieu of enforcement under paragraph (b), this subdivision may be enforced by imposition of a civil penalty and an action for damages for littering under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.99">115A.99</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.85.20.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/40">6462</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/40">6467-6</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/40">6471</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/40">6490</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/40">6496</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/40">6500</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/40">6504</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/40">6508</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/40">RL s 2500</a>,2503; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1905/0/297/#laws.0.5.0">1905 c 297 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1911/0/259/#laws.0.5.0">1911 c 259 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1911/0/355/#laws.0.5.0">1911 c 355 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1913/0/361/#laws.0.5.0">1913 c 361 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1923/0/430/#laws.0.10.0">1923 c 430 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1933/0/396/#laws.0.6.0">1933 c 396 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/525/#laws.0.3.0">1969 c 525 s 3</a>,4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/168/#laws.0.1.0">1975 c 168 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/248/#laws.0.70.0">1985 c 248 s 70</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/412/#laws.0.1.0">1994 c 412 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/351/#laws.0.2.0">2002 c 351 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/2/#laws.4.4.0">2003 c 2 art 4 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/28/#laws.1.9.0">2003 c 28 art 1 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/272/#laws.0.19.0">2012 c 272 s 19</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.85.205">
        <h1 class="shn">85.205 RECEPTACLES FOR RECYCLING.</h1>
        <p>(a) The commissioner of natural resources must provide recycling conveniences at all state parks.</p>
        <p>(b) State park managers must provide and maintain adequate receptacles for collection of food containers for recycling in all state parks.</p>
        <p>(c) Appropriate recycling information must be available to all state park visitors.</p>
        <p>(d) State park managers must post a notice of recycling availability at appropriate locations within each state park.</p>
